Safety definitions and counts

Safety describes the known effect, not whether zipmi permits execution. When one recovered firmware row combines behaviors, the displayed class is the highest known impact and the row says so.

Read-Only 119 operations

Reads state and has no known persistent or service effect.

Sensitive 9 operations

Reads or changes credentials, trust, sessions, raw memory, firmware-control state, or other security-sensitive material.

State-Changing 93 operations

Changes configuration or runtime state without a known service interruption or data deletion.

Disruptive 8 operations

Can interrupt a service, session, device, or management connection.

Destructive 12 operations

Can erase data, reset configuration, or replace firmware or persistent state.

Unknown 35 operations

Available evidence does not establish the external effect.

Inventory scope and known gaps

The 276 operation contracts cover 58 multiplexed addresses; they are not the full dispatch denominator. The companion compact table retains all 293 firmware registrations and 271 unique NetFn/Cmd identities. Exactly 43 contracts retain undetermined request and response layouts. No iDRAC9 operation has a proven exact payload codec or length bound in zipmi, so every named route requires --unsafe.

Live evidence

All 276 contracts retain the structured sweep result from 10.0.9.9: 267 were confirmed as real dispatch paths and nine returned ABSENT. A live response proves only the recorded request; it is not permission to replay state-changing or destructive commands.

1CmdOEMChassisIdentify
00/04

OEM override of the standard IPMI Chassis Identify command (netfn 0x00, cmd 0x04). Thin wrapper that tail-calls the real handler via the DellCmdChassisIdentify function pointer to blink/control the chassis identify LED. The only branch in the decompile is the compiler-inserted stack-canary check (__stack_chk_guard comparison), NOT a functional platform-context check; the wrapper itself does not parse the request bytes.

Unknown

External effect is not established.

zipmi oem idrac9 --unsafe CmdOEMChassisIdentify_00_04 <remaining payload bytes>

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: standard IPMI Chassis Identify request (parsed by the DellCmdChassisIdentify delegate, not this wrapper): req[8]=interval_seconds (0=off); req[9]=force_identify_flag -- inferred from IPMI spec, undetermined in this wrapper

Request fields

Status: Partial

Summary: from DellCmdChassisIdentify; CC 0x00 on success. The __stack_chk_fail path is standard stack-smashing detection inserted by the compiler, not a functional error path.

00/0a

Enable/disable the physical front-panel power button (per symbol name and iDRAC10 analogue). No iDRAC9 decompiled C was recovered for this handler; functional detail below is undetermined and any prior specifics were iDRAC10-derived.

3DellCmdSetSysInfo/0x00
06/58 data 00

Toggle the power-button-disable global flag. req[8]=0x00, req[7]=2 (len). If param_1[9] (first data byte after selector) is 1 and a 'CMC-notified' flag is already set, returns CC 0x81 (busy/not allowed). If param_1[9] is 0 or 1, writes the value to a global byte flag. Values >= 2 → CC unspecified (falls to default CC 0xcc path). Source: libipmicmdtableapi.so.9.9.9 addr 0x4449cd3c (dispatched via liboemcmds.so.9.9.9 registration).

State-Changing
Safety details

Conservative classification from recovered behavior; exact payload codec is not proven.

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: req[7]=2; req[8]=0x00 (selector); req[9]=power_button_disable (0=enable, 1=disable).

Request fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: resp_len=0; CC 0x00 on success; CC 0x81 if disable requested (req[9]==1) while CMC-notified flag is set; CC 0xc7 (199) if req[7]!=2; CC 0xcc if req[9]>=2 (LAB_4449d580).

4DellCmdSetSysInfo/0x02
06/58 data 02

BIOS→BMC generic system info write (type 0x02). Calls FUN_44497ef8 to parse and store the system info record. On success additionally ORs a flag byte in SHM region 0x27 offset 0x2e4 with bit 0, then sets IMC status bit 0x100000. Source: libipmicmdtableapi.so.9.9.9.

5DellCmdSetSysInfo/0x03
06/58 data 03

BIOS→BMC generic system info write (type 0x03). Identical to 0x02 but does not write the SHM flag byte. Calls FUN_44497ef8 then sets IMC status bit 0x100000. Case 0x01 aliases here (goto LAB_4449d2c8). Source: libipmicmdtableapi.so.9.9.9.

6DellCmdSetSysInfo/default
06/58 data 80

Catch-all: selectors with an empty 'break' case fall out of the switch to 'uVar20 = 0x80' (command/parameter not supported). Selectors 0xcc, 0xce, 0xcf, 0xd9, 0xdd, 0xe6, 0xeb, 0xec, 0xed, 0xf6 explicitly goto LAB_4449d218 → CC 0x82. All selectors with real handlers are documented as their own DellCmdSetSysInfo/* entries. Source: libipmicmdtableapi.so.9.9.9.

7DellCmdSetSysInfo/0xc1
06/58 data c1

PCIe inventory-lock check. If the CMC PCIe inventory-in-progress flag is set, logs a message and returns WITHOUT modifying the CC — so the selector value 0xc1 is returned as the completion code (the handler does not set 0xcc here). Otherwise falls through to FUN_44497ef8 (LAB_4449d2a0). Source: libipmicmdtableapi.so.9.9.9.

State-Changing
Safety details

Conservative classification from recovered behavior; exact payload codec is not proven.

8DellCmdSetSysInfo/0xc2
06/58 data c2

Set PCIe slot topology configuration (fpSetIPMIConfigAttrs). The lockdown path at the function entry also handles this selector: when IsSystemLockdownEnabled and selector>5, only specific slot-topology type/width values are accepted (validated bitmask at 0x2b200001); invalid types → CC 0xd4 (lockdown reject). On the non-lockdown path, validates slot type+width (CONCAT11(param_1[9],param_1[10])) against an allowlist: accepted values include {0x02, 0x03, 0x08, 0x100, 0x108, 0x200, 0x208, 0x308, 0x400, 0x800, 0x1000, 0x2000, 0x4000, 0x8000, 0x10000}; all others → CC 0xcc. Also validates slot state byte at param_1[0x13] (must be 1 or 2). Calls fpSetIPMIConfigAttrs(param_1+9, data_len-1) and returns its CC. Source: libipmicmdtableapi.so.9.9.9.

State-Changing
Safety details

Conservative classification from recovered behavior; exact payload codec is not proven.

zipmi oem idrac9 --unsafe DellCmdSetSysInfo0xc2 <remaining payload bytes>

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: req[7]=data_len; req[8]=0xc2; req[9..10]=slot_type_width (u16 LE, validated against allowlist); req[0xd..0xe]=bus_device (u16 LE; if invalid loads from existing config); req[0x13]=slot_state (1 or 2; else clamped to 2); req[9+..] = full slot record passed to fpSetIPMIConfigAttrs.

Request fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

No in-band gate. An Admin LAN caller can overwrite PCIe slot topology records.

Confidence: medium

9DellCmdSetSysInfo/0xc3
06/58 data c3

Write system GUID and associated system info record. req[7] must equal 0x12 (18 bytes). If param_1[9]==0x10, calls SetSysGUID(param_1+10) to store the 16-byte GUID, then calls writeSysInfo(param_1+9, len-1, 0xc3) to persist the record. CC is OR of both operations. req[7]!=0x12 → CC 0xc7 (LAB_4449e0ac); req[9]!=0x10 → CC 0xcc (LAB_4449d580). Source: libipmicmdtableapi.so.9.9.9.

State-Changing
Safety details

Conservative classification from recovered behavior; exact payload codec is not proven.

zipmi oem idrac9 --unsafe DellCmdSetSysInfo0xc3 <remaining payload bytes>

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: req[7]=0x12; req[8]=0xc3; req[9]=0x10 (GUID type); req[10..25]=16-byte GUID (raw binary).

Request fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: resp_len=0; CC 0x00 on full success; CC 0xff if SetSysGUID fails; CC from writeSysInfo if that fails; CC 0xc7 (199, LAB_4449e0ac) if req[7]!=0x12; CC 0xcc (LAB_4449d580) if req[9]!=0x10.

Response fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

Privilege: Admin (4)

Available on this firmware: Observed by the live firmware sweep

Captured live request: LIVE-DISPATCHED; host 10.0.9.9; CC 0xc7; data request data length invalid

Recovered from

11DellCmdSetSysInfo/0xc5
06/58 data c5

Update system node ID. Gated on IsMsgFromSystemInterface (in-band only) AND pre-POST (POST status byte not 0x51/0x7e/0x7f). Reads existing node ID from cfgdb, calls writeSysInfo(type=0xc5) and updateSystemNodeID, and if the value changed, triggers createRSPIFlashBackupThread and updates the 'previous' cfgdb attribute. OR-sets bit 2 in SHM region 0x27 offset 0x2e4. Source: libipmicmdtableapi.so.9.9.9.

State-Changing
Safety details

Conservative classification from recovered behavior; exact payload codec is not proven.

zipmi oem idrac9 --unsafe DellCmdSetSysInfo0xc5 <remaining payload bytes>

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: req[8]=0xc5; req[9..]=node ID payload (passed to writeSysInfo). Gate: IsMsgFromSystemInterface must be non-zero (in-band only) when POST is not complete.

Request fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: resp_len=0; CC 0x00 on success; CC from writeSysInfo or updateSystemNodeID on failure; falls to default (CC 0x00) if gate not met.

06/58 data c9

17DellCmdSetSysInfo/0xd6

36DellCmdSetSysInfo/0xfa
06/58 data fa

PCIe slot assignment record write (batch). Both the lockdown and non-lockdown paths route to the same handler (LAB_4449da6c). req[7] must equal 0x11 (17 bytes; else CC 0xc7). param_1[9..10] = total_count (u16 LE, must be <= 40; else CC 0xcc). param_1[11..12] = current_index (1-indexed u16 LE, must be <= total_count and > 0; else CC 0xcc). Acquires a pthread_mutex, writes the 16-byte record from param_1[13..28] into a slot array at offset (current_index-1)*0x10. If current_index==total_count (last record): iterates all records counting those with bit 6 set in byte 7, then calls DellModPostCMCPCIeReInit_IPCClient(count). Source: libipmicmdtableapi.so.9.9.9.

37DellCmdSetSysInfo/0xfe
06/58 data fe

Set boot mode/NIC flag. Reads cfgdb attribute and SHM region 0x27 offset 4 for current boot flag state. req[7] must equal 6 (else CC 0xc7). param_1[10..11] = capability_mask (u16 LE); if capability_mask == 0, falls to a path that sets boot-mode to param_1[10] (FUN_44497de0 for defaults if param_1[10]==0). Validates that requested mode (param_1[10]) is within capability_mask (bitwise AND check; else CC 0xcc). Handles NIC-pair swap (if SHM flags indicate bonded mode, swaps bit 1 and bit 2 of the mode value). Calls CfgSetAttributeInt to persist boot mode. Source: libipmicmdtableapi.so.9.9.9.

90CmdOEMSetCCRAutoSyncState
18/8c

Persists the CCR (Config Capture/Restore) auto-sync flag to cfgdb via CfgSetAttributeInt, writing the sync_state byte (req+9) as the attribute value. Side-effect: if sync_state==0 AND the internal UEFI flag (MaserCmdsGetUEFIFlag) equals 7, the UEFI flag is cleared to 0 via MaserCmdsSetUEFIFlag(0). On CfgSetAttributeInt failure the error is logged (log_dbg_msg/dellprintf) and the handler's return register (r0 = uVar2) is set to 1; on success r0 = 0.

State-Changing
Safety details

Conservative classification from recovered behavior; exact payload codec is not proven.

zipmi oem idrac9 --unsafe CmdOEMSetCCRAutoSyncState <remaining payload bytes>

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: Handler only reads req+9. req+7 is the request data-length field by this library's convention (sibling CmdOEMGetUEFIFlag validates req+7), but this handler does NOT read or validate it — no length check exists. req+8 (data[0]) is not read: meaning undetermined. req+9 (data[1]) = sync_state: 0x00 disables CCR auto-sync (and may clear the UEFI flag, see purpose); non-zero enables it. The written cfgdb attribute value is this raw byte.

Request fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: resp_len (param_2) is hardcoded to 2. Response data buffer (param_3): byte0=0x00, byte1=0x00, both hardcoded; their semantic meaning is undetermined (not derived from any input). Completion code is the handler's return value in r0, not a response-data byte: 0x00 on success, 0x01 on CfgSetAttributeInt failure. (Ghidra typed this function as void and rendered the live r0 value as an argument to __stack_chk_fail; the CC-in-r0 ABI is confirmed by sibling handler CmdOEMGetUEFIFlag, which returns its CC via r0 and uses param_3 purely for response data.)

Response fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

Privilege: Operator (3)

Interface: Out-of-band or system interface, subject to operation gates

Available on this firmware: Cataloged statically; live target reported the operation absent

Completion codes: See response summary; not separately normalized

Named operation route: Requires --unsafe

Captured live request: ABSENT; host 10.0.9.9; CC 0xc1; data invalid command

Recovered from

Library: libmaser; backend dependencies: CfgSetAttributeInt (cfgdb attribute write; attribute id resolved at link-time via DAT_443ae4c4+0x443ae3f0). MaserCmdsGetUEFIFlag / MaserCmdsSetUEFIFlag (intra-lib UEFI state accessors). log_dbg_msg / dellprintf (error logging on cfgdb-write failure).; security analysis: Operator-level (priv 3) write to a cfgdb attribute governing CCR/UEFI auto-sync state. When sync_state=0, silently resets the internal UEFI flag (7->0) via MaserCmdsSetUEFIFlag(0), which could interfere with an in-progress UEFI-update or config-restore workflow. No request-length validation is performed (req+7 ignored), unlike the sibling GetUEFIFlag handler which enforces a length of 6. On CfgSetAttributeInt failure the handler does return an error CC (r0=1), so backend write failure is surfaced to the caller.

Confidence: medium

91DellCmdGetMgrCertFingerprint
2c/01

Return the SHA-256 fingerprint of the iDRAC management certificate. Reads a 64-character ASCII hex string from cfgdb, converts it to 32 raw bytes, and returns it. Feature must be enabled in cfgdb. Requires command to arrive over KCS (in-band). Only fingerprint type 0x01 (SHA-256) is supported.

92DellCmdGetBootstrapCredentials
2c/02

Return a one-time set of bootstrap iDRAC credentials (username + password, 16 bytes each) generated via IPC. Used during OS-to-BMC provisioning flows (e.g. iSM). Feature must be enabled in cfgdb. By default, retrieving credentials atomically disables the feature (one-shot); if req[9]==0xa5 the feature-enabled flag is NOT cleared after retrieval, allowing repeated use. Requires the command to arrive over KCS (in-band system interface).

Sensitive
Safety details

Conservative classification from recovered behavior; exact payload codec is not proven.

zipmi oem idrac9 --unsafe DellCmdGetBootstrapCredentials <remaining payload bytes>

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: req+7=2 (2 data bytes). req[8]=0x52 ('R', get operation; other → CC 0xcc). req[9]=flags: 0xa5 → keep feature enabled after retrieval (non-destructive read); any other value → disables the bootstrap-credentials feature flag in cfgdb after retrieval (one-shot).

Request fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: 33 bytes (resp_len=0x21) on success. resp[0]=0x52 (completion tag). resp[1:16]=16-byte bootstrap username. resp[17:32]=16-byte bootstrap password. The last byte of each 16-byte block (resp[16] and resp[32]) must be '\0' (null-terminator check on the IPC output); if either is non-zero the handler returns CC 0xff (0xe1 is only a dellprintf log argument, NOT the returned CC). CC 0xd4 if not KCS; CC 0xc7 if req+7 != 2; CC 0xcc if req[8] != 0x52; CC 0x80 if feature-enabled flag is 0 or CfgGetAttributeInt fails; CC 0xff if GenerateBootstrapCredentials_IPCClient fails (0xdc is only a log argument) OR if the null-terminator check fails. Note: a failed CfgSetAttributeInt (flag-clear) is logged but does NOT change the CC — creds are still returned.

Response fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

Privilege: Admin (4)

Interface: System interface only (KCS/in-band)

Available on this firmware: Cataloged statically; live target reported the operation absent

Completion codes: 0xcc, 0xff, 0xd4, 0xc7, 0x80

Named operation route: Requires --unsafe

Captured live request: ABSENT; host 10.0.9.9; CC 0xc1; data invalid command

Recovered from

Library: libipmicmdtableapi; backend dependencies: IsKcsCommand (channel gate). CfgGetAttributeInt / CfgSetAttributeInt (cfgdb: bootstrap-credentials-enabled flag). GenerateBootstrapCredentials_IPCClient (IPC: 0x24-byte output buffer, generates username+password pair).; security analysis: Returns plaintext credentials over in-band IPMI. An OS-level attacker with KCS access and Admin IPMI privilege can retrieve a valid iDRAC account credential, pivoting to full BMC access. The one-shot flag (default behaviour clears the enable flag) limits exposure window but the disarm only happens on read—if cfgdb write fails the flag is not cleared and the creds remain valid (the decompiled code logs the failure but proceeds to return credentials: line 43 `local_4c = iVar1` reassigns before continuing). Admin IPMI from OS is a known privilege-escalation vector (iSM abuse).

Confidence: high

104DellCmdGetIMCStatusRegister
30/90

Read the 48-bit IMC inter-controller status register. Modular-platform only (platform_type==2). Acquires a semaphore (100 ms timeout; CC=0xc3 on timeout). Returns the current 48-bit status value and the 48-bit previous-status value. If bit 0 (IMC-ready) of current status is set, creates /tmp/imcready_read. 106DellCmdEDIDInfo
30/92

Deliver EDID (Extended Display Identification Data) for the local KVM display from the CMC. CMC-channel only (channel>>4==7; else CC=0xc1). Storage path requires req[7]==0x81 and req[8]!=0; when req[8]==0 the command is a no-op that clears IMC status bit 0x800 and returns CC=0x00. On the storage path validates a single-byte checksum over req[9..0x87] (127 bytes) against req[0x88]; on success copies the EDID block into G_sEDIDLKVM and fires a SetGPTaskEvent to notify the VKVM subsystem, then clears IMC status bit 0x800. 113DellCmdGetLastPostCode
30/99

Calls DellGetPOSTCodeInfo to look up the description. Returns the numeric code byte, string length, and string (max 198 bytes). If DellGetPOSTCodeInfo returns non-zero, returns only the code byte with zero string length. 118DellCmdSpecialACCycle
30/9f

Initiates a virtual AC power cycle on non-modular platforms, only when pre-POST. get_platform_type: if type==1 (modular/blade) → CC 0xcb. Gate is IsKcsCommand(param_1) (KCS/system interface = in-band; if 0 → CC 0xc1). Reads POST status from SHM region 0x25 offset 0xab5; if POST done (0x51 / 0x7e..0x7f) → CC 0xff. Otherwise SetSystemPowerOff(1), VirtualAcCycleReq_IPCClient, then utl_execmd spawns a task binary (path is an unresolved DAT ref; iDRAC10 shows /usr/bin/jssmtask — unverified here). Async; returns immediately. Decompiled from iDRAC9 libipmicmdtableapi.so.9.9.9 addr 0x444ade24 size 0x234.

Disruptive
Safety details

Conservative classification from recovered behavior; exact payload codec is not proven.

260CmdOEMSetMASERAccessState
30/af

Enable or disable the MASER/Lifecycle Controller access state. State 0 (disable): if current config state==2, touches/removes a recovery marker file and calls run_verify_maser_as_root, then ClearMASERInRecovery + ClearUEFIOOBRecovery + fires a DCLCLWRAPSubmitEvent. State 1 (enable): calls ClearMASERInRecovery + ClearUEFIOOBRecovery, deactivates LC timer, clears shared-mem LC status flags, fires DCLCLWRAPSubmitEvent. Persists new state via CfgSetAttributeInt.

Stores the 8 request data bytes into the G_au8CMCFeatureSupport global. Special-case write to SHM region 0x22 (large data area), accessible only via direction=2 + region=9. Used by CMC to write chassis data directly to the alternate SHM region. Any other region value with direction=2 falls through to CC 0xc9.

State-Changing

Writes directly to SHM region 0x22 regardless of channel (the bVar12==2 path does not check req[0]>>4 and has no IMC-status side-effect). Could be used by an Admin attacker to overwrite SHM 22 content directly.

Confidence: high

272DellCmdiDracPOSTCode
30/d4

Read the current BIOS POST-code status dword from shared-memory segment 10. Handler gates on a 4-byte request data field: *(int*)(req+8) (the first 4 request-data bytes as a 32-bit LE integer) must equal 0, else CC=0xcc. On pass, reads 4 bytes from SHM segment 10 offset 0 via Dell_shm_memread(10, resp_data, 0, 4). If bit 14 (0x4000) of the returned dword is set, side-effects IMC state by calling DellClearIMCPrevStatusBit(0x8000000, 0). Sets response length to 0x10 and CC=0 on success. Source: iDRAC9 liboemcmds.so.9.9.9 decompiled C at 0x4457b204 (decomp/liboemcmds/DellCmdiDracPOSTCode.c).

State-Changing
Safety details

Conservative classification from recovered behavior; exact payload codec is not proven.

zipmi oem idrac9 --unsafe DellCmdiDracPOSTCode <remaining payload bytes>

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: Handler gate (from C): *(int*)(req+8) — request data bytes [0..3] as a 32-bit LE integer — must equal 0; non-zero -> CC=0xcc. No request-length (req[7]) check exists in the decompiled handler; expected data length is undetermined from this C.

Request fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: resp_len set to 0x10 (16) on success; resp_data[0..3] = POST-code status dword written by Dell_shm_memread(seg 10, offset 0, len 4, LE); resp_data[4..15] not written by the handler (buffer contents undetermined). On error paths resp_len is left at its initial 0. CC=0x00 success; CC=0xff if Dell_shm_memread returns non-zero; CC=0xcc if *(int*)(req+8) != 0.

Response fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

Privilege: Admin (4)

Interface: Out-of-band or system interface, subject to operation gates

Available on this firmware: Observed by the live firmware sweep

Completion codes: 0xcc, 0x00, 0xff

Named operation route: Requires --unsafe

Captured live request: LIVE-DISPATCHED; host 10.0.9.9; CC 0xc7; data request data length invalid

Recovered from

Library: liboemcmds; backend dependencies: Dell_shm_memread(seg=10, offset=0, len=4); DellClearIMCPrevStatusBit(0x8000000, 0) invoked only when bit 0x4000 of the SHM dword is set.; security analysis: priv Admin (4) confirmed from idrac9_runtime_dispatch.json (0x30,0xd4). No IsMsgFromSystemInterface / in-band gate is present in the handler (no channel check in C). Read is non-idempotent: when SHM dword bit 14 (0x4000) is set the handler clears IMC boot-complete bit 0x8000000, so repeated reads after POST-complete observe changed IMC state after the first call. Exposes the raw internal POST-code status dword to any Admin-level caller on any channel.

Confidence: high

273CmdOEMGetReading
30/e2

Read a Node Manager Power Tracking (NMPT) metric. Requires NMPT to be enabled (NMPTCheckEnable). The upper nibble of the first request byte selects the reading mode: 0=current, 1=average, 2=min/max. The lower nibble selects the power domain or channel. Dispatches to an internal NMPT callback resolved at runtime via a global function pointer.

Read-Only
Safety details

Conservative classification from recovered behavior; exact payload codec is not proven.

zipmi oem idrac9 --unsafe CmdOEMGetReading <remaining payload bytes>

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: Handler does NOT check req+7 (no length gate); it indexes req[8..11] directly. req[8]: bits[7:4]=reading_type (0=current→NM callback mode 1, 1=average→mode 2, 2=min/max→mode 3; other → CC 0xcc), bits[3:0]=domain/channel (only forwarded in the min/max path via req[8:9]&0xff0f; discarded in current mode where the shifted byte is passed as 0). req[9]: high byte of the min/max selector word (req[8:9]&0xff0f), used only when reading_type=2. req[10:11]: checked, must be 0x0000 (non-zero → CC 0xcc); when reading_type=1 (average) this word is forwarded as the selector.

Request fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

Status: Partial

Length: Unknown

Summary: 4 bytes on success (resp_len=4). resp[0]=echo of req[8]. resp[1]=undetermined (handler never writes it; HtoLSB16 writes only resp[2:3]). resp[2:3]=reading value, little-endian 16-bit (HtoLSB16). CC 0xd5 if NMPT not enabled OR NM callback pointer NULL OR the NM callback returns non-zero (iVar4!=0 falls through to 0xd5). CC 0xcc if reading_type>2 or req[10:11] non-zero. No 0xff path exists in this handler.

Response fields

Field widths or meanings are not fully recovered.

Privilege: Admin (4)

Interface: Out-of-band or system interface, subject to operation gates

Available on this firmware: Observed by the live firmware sweep

Completion codes: 0xcc, 0xd5

Named operation route: Requires --unsafe

Captured live request: LIVE-DISPATCHED; host 10.0.9.9; CC 0xc7; data request data length invalid

Recovered from

Library: libipmicmdtableapi; backend dependencies: NMPTCheckEnable (global enable flag check). Runtime function pointer at DAT_44ae10ec+offset (NM power tracking read callback, modes 1/2/3 for current/average/min-max). NMPTSetEnableImp indirectly depends on same SHM state.; security analysis: Admin-only read of NM power metrics. No write path. No notable exposure beyond information disclosure of server power readings.

276DellCmdSmaMbxFlag
30/fa

Return a static SMA mailbox protocol identification flag. Always responds with a single byte value 0xd5. No input is read, no backend is queried. Used by host-side agents (iSM/SMA) to confirm Dell SMA mailbox protocol availability. Decompiled from liboemcmds.so.9.9.9 addr 0x4457b5d8 size 0x68.
